Read-only; it never edits an ADR. ([Integral-Productivity/software-architecture-claude-plugin](https://github.com/Integral-Productivity/software-architecture-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/adr-audit.md))

- **advanced-code-review-plan**: 1. **Risk-based prioritization**: Higher risk files are reviewed first. Security, payment, and migration files take precedence over tests and documentation. 2. **Scope clarity**: All files in scope must be identified before review starts. No file should be discovered mid-review. 3. **Complexity honesty**: Estimates must reflect actual review effort required. Underestimating leads to rushed reviews ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/advanced-code-review-plan.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-plan**: Advanced Code Review Phase 1: Strategic Planning - scope analysis, risk categorization, priority ordering ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/advanced-code-review-plan.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-report**: Advanced Code Review Phase 5: Report Generation - produce final deliverables including Markdown report and JSON summary ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/advanced-code-review-report.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-report**: 1. **Signal over noise**: Only verified findings appear in the final report. REFUTED findings are excluded. Quality of findings matters more than quantity. 2. **Actionable output**: Every finding must have clear next steps. Findings without suggestions or context are not actionable. 3. **Machine-readable artifacts for automation**: JSON summary enables CI/CD integration, automated triage, and tool ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/advanced-code-review-report.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-review**: Perform multi-pass code analysis, generate findings with severity classification, and respect previous review context. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/advanced-code-review-review.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-review**: Advanced Code Review Phase 3: Deep Review - multi-pass code analysis, finding generation with severity classification ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/advanced-code-review-review.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-verify**: 1. **Every finding must be verifiable against actual code**: If a finding cannot be verified by reading the file at the specified line, it is not a valid finding. 2. **REFUTED findings must be removed, not just flagged**: False positives erode trust. Remove them from final output entirely; log in audit for transparency. 3. **INCONCLUSIVE findings must be clearly marked**: Uncertainty is acceptable ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/advanced-code-review-verify.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-verify**: Advanced Code Review Phase 4: Verification - fact-check findings against codebase, remove false positives ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/advanced-code-review-verify.md) · ★13)

- **aedl-maintain**: Run a numbered maintenance pass over the agentic harness (aedl -maintain) — mine observer/blocked logs, worklogs, exchange notes, and issues into evidence-backed findings, then fix them under the harness's own rules and record docs/maintenance/maintenance-log-&lt;N&gt;.md. --dry-run = findings only. ([matterjd/jigbench](https://github.com/matterjd/jigbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/aedl-maintain.md))
- **aedl-observe**: Set up and operate the read-only observation kit (aedl -observe) — time logs + portable memory observations written ONLY to a standalone kit directory, modeled on the T4 readonly/logging tier. Subcommands - setup (guided terminal wizard, non-expert friendly), log (end-of-session entry + memory harvest), status (kit health, writes nothing). Claude-agnostic kit; provably non-intrusive. ([matterjd/jigbench](https://github.com/matterjd/jigbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/aedl-observe.md))
- **aedl-review-cycle**: Drive a PR through the Copilot review loop (aedl -review-cycle) — request a Copilot review, watch for comments, delegate a scoped fix pass under a signed grant, verify green, then request merge authorization. Never auto-merges. Resumable per PR. ([matterjd/jigbench](https://github.com/matterjd/jigbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/aedl-review-cycle.md))
- **aedl-roadmap**: Reconcile open issues against roadmap surfaces — orphan issues, orphan rows, stale-delivered rows, zombie rows — across one repo or all registered ones. Read-first, one batched approval round, never closes an issue (aedl -roadmap). ([matterjd/jigbench](https://github.com/matterjd/jigbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/aedl-roadmap.md))
- **aedl-skill**: Scaffold a new @aedl command — generate a conventions-compliant skill, its /aedl-&lt;name&gt; slash command, and the AGENTS.md + CLAUDE.md routing rows in one pass. Leaves files untracked (no auto-commit). ([matterjd/jigbench](https://github.com/matterjd/jigbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/aedl-skill.md))
- **aedl-verify**: AEDL stage 8 — the verification pipeline. Deterministic gates, then four independent review axes (Standards · Spec · Security · Design) in parallel sub-agents scaled to the slice's autonomy tier, then one triaged ranked findings list (aedl -verify). ([matterjd/jigbench](https://github.com/matterjd/jigbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/aedl-verify.md))
